# Fallout 4 Papyrus Reference -- part 6 of 6

> Split from `FO4_Papyrus_Reference.md` by `tools/split_reference.py` because
> the whole table exceeds the retrieval index's per-file cap. Scripts in this
> part run from `MS10BillyScript` to `Scripts that mix global and member functions (for example `Game`-style additions in F4SE layers) are visible in the per-script tables via the Flags column.`.

Generated by `gen_papyrus_ref.py` from real `.psc` sources. Do not hand-edit: change the sources or the generator and rebuild.

## Plan: passes from minimal to robust

| Pass | Scope | Status |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Complete inventory of every script, function, event and property, with signatures and flags; extends tree; mechanical cross-references (who returns a type, event to register pairing) | Done |
| 2 | Behavior notes per function: latent or not, threading and save-state effects, known gotchas (from CK wiki, cited) | Not started |
| 3a | Top-down trace: Papyrus function to native binding to C++ function to engine types (CommonLibF4 sources in your RAG); coverage gaps and stubs | Done if the Pass 3a section is present (needs `--rag`) |
| 3b | Bottom-up: engine events to Papyrus events, CK surfaces (properties, fragments, terminals), console path | Not started |
| 4 | Verify: rebuild this file from your own `Data/Scripts/Source/Base`, diff against this one, then compile-test your PipMon scripts against it | Not started |

## Sources and caveats

| Layer | What it is | Source note |
|---|---|---|
| `game` |  | GAME-SHIPPED script sources, 12 files (Data\Scripts\Source, top level). The JContainer / JDB scripting framework that ships with Fallout 4 itself. |
| `ck` |  | CREATION KIT base script sources, 7,920 files (Data\Scripts\Source\Base, including Base\Fragments). THIS IS THE DECLARATION AUTHORITY for build 1.11.240.0: every Function, Event and Property attributed to this layer is declared by this build's own shipped source, not by a wiki, a mod, or a memory. If a name is not in this layer, it is not in this build. |

- Everything outside `vanilla` is **not part of the base game**: a function marked `f4se` fails at runtime if F4SE is not loaded.
- Signatures come from source, so they are exact. Descriptions are the source comments where one exists; absence of a note means the source had none.
- A signature is not proof of runtime behavior. Behavior is Pass 2; proof against your build is Pass 4.

| `AddToMap` | - | bool abAllowFastTravel = false | native | ck | Adds this reference (which is a map marker) to the map, optionally making it available for fast travel |
| `ApplyConveyorBelt` | - | string aTarget, float aLinVelX, float aLinVelY, float aLinVelZ, bool abOn = true, bool abReverse = false | native | ck | Add conveyor belt to this reference |
